[ śarīravyatirekaprakaraṇam ]
śarīradāhe pātakābhāvāt ||3|1|4||
evaṃ prathamaprakaraṇena indriyavyatireke sādhite yadi kaścicvodayet-nanu mā
bhūvannindriyāṇi cetanāni vyavasthitaviṣayatvāt, śarīraṃ tu bhaviṣyati| avyavasthita-
viṣayaṃ hi tat, yo'haṃ gauraḥ pīno yuvā saubhāgineyaḥ punaruktajanmā rūpaṃ dṛṣṭavān
so'haṃ sparśa spṛśāmītyanubhavāditi| tatra idaṃ prakaraṇāntaramupatiṣṭhate, śarīradāhe ityādi|
taccaitadasamañjasam, bhūtacaitanikānāṃ pātakābhāvābhyupagameneṣṭaprasañjanāt| ata

[p. 432]
āha-ayaṃ ceti| nanu śākyāḥ pātakamicchanto'pi na bhūtacaitanyaṃ manyante, ata
āha-evaṃ ca na buddhiriti | upalakṣaṇaṃ caitat| yadi kaścit bhūtacaitaniko'pi
pātakamicchediti | yadi kaścit śākyo'pi bhūtacaitanyamicchedityapi draṣṭavyam|
śakyate cāṅgīkārayitum, pratikṣaṇapariṇāme vilakṣaṇaśakteḥ kāryasyotpādābhyupagamāt
iti yathāśrutasūtrānurodhena vyākhyātam| vastutastu dāhena nāśamupalakṣayati, pātakā-
bhāvena ca kālāntaropayogisthāyiguṇābhāvam| śākyacārvākayoḥ sādhāraṇatvā-
ccaivamuktamiti| ata eva bhāṣyavārttikeṇa śarīrādisaṃghāteti |
nanu kimayaṃ niyama eva yat kartaryeva phalaṃ nākartarīti ? yadyevaṃ kathaṃ tarhi jāteṣṭeḥ
putragāmi phalam, putrakṛtasya śrāddhasya pitṛgāmītyata āha- śāstradeśitamiti |
yadyapi samyaggṛhasthāśramoktadharmaparipālanasya brahmalokaprāptyādikaṃ kartaryeva phalam,
tathāpi prātisvikakriyāphalābhiprāyeṇaitad draṣṭavyam| nanu na bījāvayavāḥ sarvathā
paramāṇusād bhavanti sarvāvasthopalabdherityukte pralayāvasthāyāṃ śālibījādyutpattirna
prāpnotīti deśyam, kūṣmāṇḍapatanavadityata āha- paramāṇvavasthā iti| bhājana-
gatānāmapāṃ bhājanavināśe'vasthānameva na syāditi ā dvyaṇukāt ā cāntyāvayavino
yugapadvināśe kramasyāpyasaṃbhava iti bhāvaḥ | yadavaṣṭambhenaitat
sarvamuktaṃ tat smārayati- yathā ceti ||4||
tadabhāvaḥ sātmakapradāhe'pi tannityatvāt ||3|1|5||
tada ............ tvāt ||5||
na kāryāśrayakartṛvadhāt ||3|1|6||

[ cakṣuradvaitaprakaraṇam ]
savyadṛṣṭasyetareṇa pratyabhijñānāt ||3|1|7||
evamanena prakaraṇena śarīravyatirekaṃ tulyanyāyatayā buddhivyatirekaṃ ca prasādhya
prakaraṇāntaramavatārayati- itaśceti | taccānupapannam, anena hīndriyavyatirekaḥ
prasādhyaḥ| sa ca
darśanasparśanābhyāmekārthagrahaṇāt
ityādinā prathamaprakaraṇenaiva prasādhitaḥ| samuccayārthaṃ bhaviṣyatītyetadapi nāsti
samuccīyamānārthāsaṃbhavasya vārttikakṛtā darśitatvāt| na, indriyārthavyatireka-
prasādhanaprasaṅgena saṃbhāvitopanyāsasya prāmāṇikatayā ca varjanīyasyārthasyopekṣā-
narhatvādavatāraḥ ||
savya...t|| vyavasāyānuvyavasāyarūpapratyabhijñānadvayaṃ vyākhyānena bhinatti- tatra
mānasamiti| tadetat prakaraṇaṃ vārttikakāro dūṣayatīti| yathāśrutamiti śeṣaḥ|
pratisandhānābhāsanirākaraṇārthaṃ tvasyārambha iti darśitameva| nanu sakalākṣo dvābhyāṃ
paśyati, vikalākṣastvekeneti | tat ko'yaṃ vikalāvikalākṣayostulyopalambhaprasaṅga
ityata āha- ayamabhisandhiriti | nanu cakṣuḥ śabdasya jātivacanatve ghrāṇādiśabdā
api tatheti kuto vyaktiśabdānāṃ jātiśabdasamabhivyāhārānupapattirata āha-na ca
tadekasmin śarīra iti| ekaikaṃ śarīramadhikṛtya cintāyāṃ ghrāṇādayo na sāmānyaśabdāḥ
kiṃ tu vyaktiśabdāṃ eveti vyavasthitamiti bhāvaḥ ||7||

na smṛteḥ smartavyaviṣayatvāt ||3|1|13 ||
nanvindriyavikārādityanena vikārecchāsmṛtisaṃskārānubhavānāṃ kramānumitā-
nāmekamadhikaraṇamanumīyate pramātṛbhede pratisandhānādarśanāt| na tu smṛtiviṣayatayā
ātmānumīyata iti darśitam| tat kutastyo'yamupālambhaḥ smṛteḥ smartavyaviṣaya-
tvāditi ? ataḥ pūrvapakṣiṇo'bhimānamāha- smṛtirātmānamiti || 13 ||
tadātmaguṇasadbhāvādapratiṣedhaḥ ||3|1|14||
na sarvāsu smṛtiṣu jñātrādikaṃ parisphuratītyato viśinaṣṭi- mānasānuvyavasā-
yeti| nanvaviśiṣṭārthatve'viśiṣṭajātīyatve ca smṛtīnāṃ caturdhā pradarśanaṃ kimartha-
mityata āha-ekatretyādi | yadyevaṃ kathaṃ caturvidhamapyetad vākyaṃ samānārthamiti
bhāṣyamata āha-jñātṛjñeyeti| nanu pratyakṣe'rthe smṛtiriti vyāhatamata āha-smṛtiriti
pratyabhijñānamāheti| nanu bālavṛddhaśarīrayoravasthe eva paraṃ bhidyete nāvasthāva-
tpratyabhijñānādata āha- anyaddhīti| yatrāpi kathañcit pratyabhijñānaṃ tatrāpi viruddha-
parimāṇādhyāsena bhedasya tāttvikatayā tadapramāṇam| na ca rūpabhedavat parimāṇabhedo'-
pyekatra saṃbhaviṣyatīti vācyam, pūrvottarayorvināśotpādakāraṇābhāvāt, āśrayanāśasya
kāraṇagatasaṃkhyāparimāṇapracayānāṃ ca tathābhāvāt| anyathā tvatiprasaṅgādityarthaḥ |

niyamaśca niranumānaḥ ||3|1|17||
tacca liṅgajamiti | tacceti tadapītyarthaḥ | yasya tu manaḥpratyakṣamiti |
acintyo hi yogaprabhāvātiśaya iti kecit| tadasat| na hi yogaprabhāvena
viruddhānāmarthānāṃ virodho nivāryate, kiṃ tu kāryakāraṇasvabhāvānatikrameṇa tataḥ sarvatra
śakterapratighātādityasmadādito viśeṣaḥ| tasmāt mano mano'ntareṇa pratyakṣamīkṣate |
ato na karmakaraṇavirodha ityapare | vyaktaṃ tu ṭīkākṛtā asmadādimanojñānatulyatayā
tadapi parihṛtam| yathā hi svarūpeṇa mano jñeyaṃ liṅgajñānasahitaṃ tu jñaptikaraṇamasmadādīnām,
tathā yogināmapi svarūpeṇa jñeyaṃ yogajadharmānugṛhītaṃ tu jñaptikaraṇamiti viśiṣṭā-
viśiṣṭabhedena na kaścid virodha iti syāt| idaṃ tu cintanīyam, ko'yamekatra
karmakaraṇavirodho nāma ? kartṛvyāpāryatvaṃ karaṇatvam, anātmasamavetakriyāphalaśālitvaṃ
ca karmatvam| na ca kartṛvyāpāryasya parasamavetakriyāphalaśālitāyāṃ kiñcit sva-
rūpataḥ phalato vā virudhyate| virudhyata eva yadā tayaivāsidhārayā tadasidhārācchedaḥ
prasajyata iti cet-na, kāraṇābhāvāt| prāptā hyasidhārā cchinatti, nāprāptā| prāptiśca
bhedādhiṣṭhānā nābhede bhavitumarhatīti| na cehāpi tathā| na hi liṅgamindriyaprāptimapekṣate,
kiṃ tu vyāptim| yogipratyakṣe tu cetasi  deśyametadavakāśamāsādayet| prāptaṃ hi cetaḥ
pratyakṣatāmiyāt| na ca tenaiva tatprāpyata iti cet - na, yogipratyakṣe yogajadharmānugraha-
lakṣaṇāyāḥ prāpteḥ prāpyantarasyāvivakṣitatvāt| anyathā atītānāgatāvedanaprasaṅgāt|
etena kartṛkarmāvirodho'pi vyākhyātaḥ| enamevārthamunnetuṃ ṭīkeyamīdṛgiti

śarīradāhe pātakābhāvāt
ityādinā janmanaḥ pūrvaṃ prāyaṇāccordhvamātmāstītyeṣo'pyartho nirṇīta eveti cetna,

iha ca paraloka eva sādhyata iti viśeṣaḥ | yadyapi dehabhedāditi bhāṣyamanyathāpi

pratisandhātṛtvāditi, tathāpi sugamatayā anyathā vyācaṣṭe - lyablope pañcamīti |
pū... tteḥ || abhipretaviṣayaprārthanāprāptaviti vārttikam| taccānupapannam|
prārthanāprāptiśabdayordvandvakarmadhārayasamāsānupapattau tatpuruṣasamāsaḥ syāt| tatra ca
prārthanāgrahaṇam niṣprayojanam, prārthanāmantareṇāpyabhimataviṣayaprāptimātreṇa harṣotpatteḥ |
ata āha- prārthanāgrahaṇamiti | hānāśakyateti | hānāśakyatā heyadharmaḥ| bhayaṃ tu
pratipattṛdharmaḥ | na cāśakyateti padavipariṇāme'pi sattāmātreṇa tat kamparomāñcādikaṃ
janayati| bhayaṃ ca loke kamparomāñcādijanakaṃ pratipattṛdharma iti prasiddhamata āha-
viṣayeṇeti | nanu prāptumaśakyasyeṣṭasya prārthanā yadi śokaḥ syāt paṇḍakasyāpi mo-

svābhāvike pratibandhe piśācāyamānaḥ kaścidupādhiriti | tadutthāpya dūṣayati - syā-
detaditi| tathāpi prakārāntarāniṣedhe tadāśritya sthirāṇāmarthakriyākaraṇānna viśva-
kṣaṇabhaṅgasiddhiḥ| na ca tatsaṃbhāvanāyāṃ dṛśyamānānāmapyarthakriyāṇāṃ kramayaugapadya-
vyāptisiddhiḥ, tenāpi tannirvāhādata āha- na ceti| yadyapi cātrāpi prayoge sādhya-
vikalo dṛṣṭāntaḥ, niṣedhyasya prakārāntarasyādṛśyatayā nīlānīlayorapi niṣedhāsaṃbha-
vāt, tādātmyamātraniṣedhe ca siddhasādhanāt, tathāpi mitho vidhiniṣedhayoḥ 'prakārāntara-
yostṛtīyaprakārāśaṅkāyāṃ svavāgvirodhaḥ syāditi tarkapuraskārāt sudṛḍhaṃ pratyakṣame-
katretyanyatrānumānatayāpyucyata iti na kaścid doṣaḥ| saha kurvanto hi sahakāriṇa
ityucyante| nāpakāraṃ kurvantaḥ| tathā cānupakārakā api sahakāriṇa iti kimanupapannam?
tathā ca sāmagrīsaṃpādyaṃ kāryamekasmāt saṃbhāvayantaḥ kāraṇasvabhāvamevāvijñātavantaḥ
pare iti trilocanaḥ| tadutthāpya dūṣayati- yadi manyeteti| nanūktaṃ sāmagrī-
sampādyamityādi | tatrāha- na ceti| bhūṣaṇamutthāpya dūṣayati - eteneti| nanu kiṃ
tadakṣaṇikaṃ yataḥ kramayaugapadyavyāvṛtteḥ sattvavyāvṛttirata āha- nīlameveti| kastatra
kramayaugapadyānupalambho nīlādiṣu kramayaugapadyopalambhādata āha - tādṛśeti | maula-
hetuvaiyarthyamācāryāpāditaṃ dūṣayati - na ceti | nanvasādhāraṇatvamubhayatrāsaṃbhavaḥ
sandigdhavyatirekitvaṃ cobhayatrāpi 'saṃbhavaḥ| tat kathamidamubhayamekatretyata āha- yathā
hīti | yena hi prakāreṇa vicārābhāsārūḍhena sattvamakṣaṇikād vyāvartate tena kṣaṇikā-
dapi| pramāṇatastu naikasmādapīti bhāvaḥ| nanvevamubhayatrāsaṃbhave'rthakriyaiva na syāt|
kṣaṇikatvākṣaṇikatvābhyāṃ prakārāntarābhāvāditi prasaṅgato'pyasiddhirvaktumucitā, na
tvasādhāraṇyamata āha- tasmād gandhavattvavaditi | pakṣe pramāṇasiddhasyaiva sattvasya
sapakṣāsapakṣayorasaṃbhavenāsādhāraṇyam, anyathā gandhavattvasyāpi nityatvānityatvābhyāṃ

prakārāntarāsaṃbhavādasiddhireva syānnatvasādhāraṇatvamiti bhāvaḥ | ato'navabuddhaṭīkā-
rthena jñānaśriyā yat kiñciduktaṃ tanna lagatīti|
yat punaratroktaṃ tenaiva, ko'pekṣārthaḥ kiṃ taiḥ sahakaraṇamityanvayaḥ, atha tairvinā
akaraṇamiti vyatirekaḥ atha tairupakṛtasya karaṇamityupakāraḥ? tatra prathame sāpekṣa
eva kṣaṇiko bhāvaḥ, kārye sahakārisahitasyaiva samarthasya sattvāt| dvitīye tu tairvinā
nāstyeva kasyākaraṇamityastu, asattvādevākaraṇāt, tṛtīye tvanapekṣa eva, kṣaṇasyā-
bhedyatvenānupakāryatvāt, karaṇameva tūpakāraḥ| na ca sahakāribhistat saṃbhavati|
samānasamayatvenāviśeṣāditi nobhayaprakāravyāvṛttiḥ kṣaṇikāt| na caivamakṣaṇike'pi
saṃbhavati| tathā hi sahakāribhiḥ sahakaraṇasaṃbhave'pi tadvirahe'pi tasya bhavataḥ kutaḥ
kāryākaraṇam ? na hi kṣaṇikapakṣa iva tadabhāve tasyābhāva eva| tathā satya-
kṣaṇikatvānupapatteḥ| upakārapakṣastvasaṃbhāvita evānavasthāderiti | tadasādhīyaḥ| taiḥ
sahaiva tāvat karotītyanvayaḥ kṣaṇikapakṣe'pyavarjanīyaḥ, anyathā tairvināpi kvacit
'karaṇaprasaṅgāt| tathā ca tairvinā na karotyevetyapi vyatireko'varjanīya eva,
anyathānvayaniyamasyaivāsiddheḥ| anvayavyatirekaniyamayorekatarasiddheranyatarasiddhi-
nāntarīyakatvāt| sa cāyamevaṃbhūtaḥ svabhāvaḥ kuta āyāta ityanuyoge sāmagrī-
śaktirevottaramiti tvameva raṭasi| tathā ca śaktisvabhāvanirvacanamubhayorapyāvayoḥ
samānam| kevalaṃ kṣaṇikastairvinā samartho nāstyeva, sthirastvastītyanyadetat|
svabhāvābhyupagamastvaviśiṣṭa ityetāvanmātravyutpādanaparaścāyaṃ gabhīrataro granthamahāhṛdaḥ
so'yamuparipnavamānaiḥ kathamivāvagāhyatām ?
kiṃ ca sahaiva kurvāṇaḥ kālāntare na bhaviṣyatītyetadeva kutaḥ ? svakāraṇata eva
tatsvabhāvatvāditi cet- nanu svakāraṇavaśāt tairvinā svarūpavyāvṛttiḥ, na tu
kāryamātravyāvṛttiriti kuto niścitam ? prasaṅgatadviparyayābhyāmiti cet - nanvanena
svabhāvasandehena tayoreva vidhūnanaṃ prārabdham| na hyevaṃ svābhāvyasiddhimantareṇānyādṛśaṃ

sāmarthyamupādāya tayoḥ pravṛttiriti kiṃ nāvabudhyase? api 'ca tat kṣaṇāntare mā bhūt,

patteḥ| tādṛśatve'pi tairvinā na karotītyeva svabhāvatvenaivopapatteḥ| etacca pratyakṣasiddhameva|
na hyatādṛśatvavat tasya tādṛśatvamapi saṃdihyate| iha ca śilādivadatādṛśa-
tve'pyetadupapadyata iti śaṅkotthāpanamapi svavadhāya kṛtyotthāpanam| vyatirekabhaṅganiścaya
iva tatsandehe'pi hetuhānerupapatteḥ na ca śaṅkāpi| avanipavanapāvakādisaṃvalitāt,
śilāśakalādaṅkurānudbhavavat tanmadhyamadhiśayānād bījajātīyāt tadanāvirbhāvā-
bhāvāt| tasmāt kṣaṇikasyāpi taiḥ saha kartṛtvaṃ tairvinā akartṛkatvamityeva sva-
bhāvaḥ| evaṃ svabhāvācca kṣaṇikāt yadyanupakārakatāmātreṇa sahakāryapekṣā vyāvartate,
kṣaṇikādapi vyāvarteta, aviśeṣādityarthaḥ|

nānāsvabhāvakāryakartṛsvarūpasya svakāraṇādutpatteḥ kimanupapannam ? na hyekenaikameva
kartavyamanekenānekameveti pratibandhaḥ saugatābhyupagamaḥ samayo vā| yattu
ayameva hi bhedo bhedaheturvā yaduta viruddhadharmādhyāsaḥ kāraṇabhedo vā|
iti, tat kāryasājātyavaijātye prati sāmagrīsājātyavaijātyarūpakāraṇabhedaprayojakatva-
māśritya, na punaḥ svalakṣaṇamekaṃ santānaṃ vā, kayācit pratyāsattyā samagrāṇāmeka-
samūhatayā kāryakāraṇātmakarūpatāṃ vā, yenāsmanmatābhijñānaśauṇḍīryeṇa vācaspate-
rasmadganthasya likhanaṃ śobheta| tadiyaṃ bhedābhedānukaraṇavārtā naiyāyikānāṃ gopamukhāt
pāyasamiṣṭatāśrutimanuharati | tadetadapyanākalitaṭīkārthasya vyāhṛtam| tathā hi, na
tāvadaṅkurādīnāṃ tadatadrupatā kāraṇānapekṣā bhavitumarhati, atiprasaṅgāt| nāpyaṅkarā-
dyakartṛsvabhāvādhīnā virodhāt| tato'ṅkurakartṛsvabhāvādhīnā aṅkurasyāṅkarasvabhāvate-
tyabhyupagamanīyam| tathā ca kṣityādīnāmapi tadekasvabhāvaniṣpādyatayā aṅkuratva-

nendriyārthayostadvināśe'pi jñānāvasthānāt ||3|2|18||
evaṃ prāsaṅgikaṃ kṣaṇabhaṅgabhaṅgaprakaraṇaṃ samāpya upapannamanityā buddhiriti prakṛtaṃ
saṃsmārya tadātmaguṇatvaprakaraṇamavatārayati-cintāntaramiti | sāṃkhyābhimatanitya-
buddhitattvanirākaraṇe sati hi buddherātmaguṇatvaṃ saṃbhāvyate| tatsadbhāve jñānasya taddharmatayā
ātmadharmatvānupapatteḥ| ataḥ pūrvaprakaraṇamuttarasya hetunirvāhakaṃ ceti yukta idānīṃ
tadavatāraḥ | prayojanaṃ cāsya śarīrādivyatiriktātmasiddhireva | ātmaprakaraṇe hi prati-
sandhānabalena buddhyā ātmā sādhitaḥ| iha tu guṇatayeti viśeṣaḥ | tathāpi paraloki-
namātmānaṃ sādhayatā buddhiṃ prati kartṛtvavat śarīrādivyatiriktasyaiva smṛtisaṃskārā-
nubhavāśrayatvamapi prasādhitamevetyāśayavatā bhāṣyakṛtoktam-prasiddho'pīti | tat
tathaivāvatārayati-nanviti ||18 ||
yugapajjñeyānupalabdheśca na manasaḥ ||3|2|19||
nanu yoginaḥ puruṣāntaraparicchede manaḥ karaṇam, na puruṣāntaramata āha-
yādṛśo'yamiti | nanu jñasya vaśitvāditi buddherātmaguṇatve sādhye vyadhikaraṇo heturata
āha - kartṛkaraṇādīti | tadayamarthaḥ | cakṣurādikaraṇakarmakartṛsamudāye kartā buddhiguṇaḥ

pariṇamata iti gamyata ityarthaḥ| śarīrādyutpatteḥ pūrvaṃ pradhānādivat| jīvānāmapyacaitanyāt
tatpariṇāmamāśaṅkaya sūtrāvayavamuttarayati - puruṣaścediti| na ca so'pi cetanaḥ
kathamātmānaṃ duḥkharūpatayā pariṇamayediti vācyam, mahāvikāraśāntaye svalpa-
duḥkhopādānasyāpi darśanāt ||19||
na puruṣakarmābhāve phalāniṣpatteḥ ||4|1|20||
siddhāntasūtraṃ vyācaṣṭe - idamatrākūtamiti | etacca mokṣalakṣaṇe saprapañcaṃ vyākhyā-
tam| tattveneti bhavitari draṣṭavyam | atastacca brahmetyanena na virodhaḥ| tadvivarta

jhaṭiti aprayatnataḥ sphuranti| alpaviṣayāstu viśeṣāḥ krameṇa yatnata ityāśaya-
vatoktam - prathamamiti| vastutastu pūrvaṃ saha paścād veti na kaścid viśeṣaḥ| nanu
jñānanityatāyāṃ siddhāyāṃ viṣayabhedavyavasthā syāt| saiva tu kuta ityata āha-
vyāptibaleneti| siddhāvantarbhūyeti draṣṭavyam| vipakṣe bādhakena hyapādhiśaṅkā nivarta-
nīyā taccoktameva| acetanānāṃ svayamapravṛttau kāryānutpattiprasaṅgaḥ| tathā satyāha-
teneti| nanu nāstīha dahana iti niṣedhe nāvaśyaṃ tadviparītena jalena bhavitavyam| tat
ko'yaṃ prasaṅga ekadeśadarśipūrvakatvaṃ sādhitaṃ syāditi| na, anavabodhāt| buddhi- 20
matpūrvakatvasāmānyavivāde viśeṣaniṣedhaḥ śeṣābhyanujñāṃ paryavasyati| paśyati na veti
sandehe ca ātmānaṃ na paśyatīti yathā| anyathātve tadeva brūyāt na paśyatīti|
tathehāpi vadet na jñātṛpūrvakā iti| tacca vikalpitameveti| upalakṣaṇaṃ caitat|

[p. 525]
nanvasannakhyātigocara iti svavāgviruddham, akhyāteḥ khyātigocaratāyā api
niṣeddhamaśakyatvāt| na cātra mūkatvamātrāvalambanam| mūkatvena hi vāco nivartante na
tu pratyayā iti| na, kāladeśasvarūpaniyatasya tadaniyamena prasaṅgapravṛtteḥ | yadi hyayaṃ

yadyatrāpyasan tatrevehāpi na pratīyeta| apaṭarūpatayeva ghaṭarūpatayāpi yadyasan tadā
tadrūpatayevaitadrūpatayāpi na pratīyeta| kvacit tathā yadi na pratīyate'nyathātrāpi na
pratīyeteti yāvaduktaṃ bhavati tāvaduktaṃ bhavati asannakhyātigocara iti||
na....m|| bhāṣyoktasūtrārthatrayasya bhedaṃ vivecayati - svabhāvo dharma iti| tena
svabhāvo dharmaḥ svarūpamitararūpāsaṃkīrṇaṃ ceti| tatra prathame itaretaravyāvṛttadharmaśāli-
tvāt na tuccharūpā bhāvāḥ, tucchasya viśeṣābhāvādityarthaḥ| dvitīye tu na tuccharūpā
bhāvāḥ, anubhavavirodhādityarthaḥ | tṛtīye tu na tuccharūpā bhāvā hetusiddhyasiddhivirodhā-
dityarthaḥ| tathāpyatucchasyābhāvasāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ vyāhatamata āha - praśnapūrvakamiti |
tarkavikalpadoṣān pariharati - bhāvaśceti | kāraṇāntarāt tu vinaśyatīti vinā-
śo'sya jāyata ityarthaḥ| etadeva yattvityādinā sphuṭayati||38||
na svabhāvasiddhirāpekṣikatvāt ||4|1|39||
āpekṣikatvasya hetorbhāgāsiddhiṃ pariharati- sarva eva hīti | nanu bhavatu
bhinnatvamāpekṣikaṃ tathāpyasvābhāvikatve kimāyātamityata āha- yacca parāpekṣamiti|
idantviha vaktavyam-kiṃ parāpekṣaivāsvābhāvikatve hetuḥ| atha parasparāpekṣā, tatra
bahuvyāghātajarjaratayā prathamaḥ kalpo'tinirdala iti dvitīyo'vaśiṣyate| so'pya-
siddhaḥ| na hi ghaṭapaṭādayaḥ parasparāpekṣāḥ | nāpi daṇḍādikāraṇacakraghaṭādikārya-
nicayāḥ parasparāpekṣāḥ||39||

[p. 526]
vyāhatatvādayuktam ||4|1|40||
nanu hrasvadīrghatvaparatvāparatvādayastāvat parasparāpekṣā eva| tata eṣāmasvābhā-
vikatvaṃ prasādhya vyavahartavyatvena tānudāhṛtya sarvabhāvānāmasvābhāvikatvaṃ prasādha-
yiṣyāma ityata āha- samādhatta iti ||
vyā....m|| nanu parasparāpekṣaṃ dīrghatvaṃ hrasvatvaṃ ca dṛṣṭacaramata āha- parimāṇabhedo
hīti | autpattika iti| utpattipratibaddha ityarthaḥ| atiśayānatiśayau sāmānyaviśeṣau |
parasparagrahaṇādhīnāviti parasparagrahaṇādhīnavikalpāvityarthaḥ | tadevāha-tathā
hīti | parāpekṣaḥ parasparāpekṣa ityarthaḥ | viśeṣaṇaṃ vyavacchedako dharmo bhāvābhāvarūpa
ityarthaḥ| vastvantaraṃ pratiyoginamityarthaḥ| bhinnatvena nirūpitamiti śeṣaḥ| kathaṃ tarhi
apekṣate'ta āha- svanirūpaṇa iti| atrāpi nirūpitamiti śeṣaḥ| jananaśakti-
riti| pūrvakālaniyama ityarthaḥ| nanu yadyapi na kiñcit parasparāpekṣotpādaṃ tathāpi
paratvāparatvādayo buddhyapekṣāḥ santo na yāṃ kañcid buddhimapekṣyotpadyante, kintu
buddhiviśeṣam| tathā ca buddhiviśeṣādeva vyavahāraviśeṣo nirvastuka evāstu kṛtaṃ
paratvādinā| tadvadeva ca ghaṭatvaghaṭādivyavahārā api syurityata āha- paratvāpara-
tvādayastviti | yathā cedaṃ nirvahati tathā vaiśeṣike'dhyavaseyam| na ca dṛṣṭānta-
mātralābhena sarvamapekṣābuddhiprabhavaṃ goghaṭādestadanapekṣasyaiva utpādadarśanādityāha - na
caitāvateti | nirapekṣo'pekṣābuddhinirapekṣa ityarthaḥ| dīrghatvādayo bhaviṣyanti
vyavahartavyatāmātraśarīrā iti śeṣaḥ| svayaṃ svātantryeṇetyarthaḥ |||40||
|| iti sarvābhāvaprakaraṇam ||
[ saṃkhyaikāntaprakaraṇam ]

[p. 527]
saṅkhyaikāntāsiddhiḥ kāraṇānupapattyupapattibhyām ||4|1|41||
anubhavavyāghātāvyāghātābhyāṃ pūrvāparaparigrahaparivarjanayorānantaryamityāha-
atheti | sarvamekaṃ sadaviśeṣāditi jñānavivakṣāderārabhya sarvaṃ vyāhatamityata
āha-idamasyākūtamiti | prapañcaḥ prakāśādbhinnaḥ svayaṃ vā prakāśatām, anyena vā
prakāśeta| prathame doṣamāha- jaḍasyeti| dvitīye doṣamāha- na ceti| tatrākiñcitkarasya
tadviṣayatāyāmatiprasaṅga iti manvāna upasaṃharati- tasmāditi | na prakāśasaṃbhava
iti prasaṅgaḥ | prakāśate cāyamiti viparyayo draṣṭavyaḥ | yadi nāma ghaṭādayaḥ parasparavyāvṛttā
eva prakāśarūpā kimāyātamadvaitasyetyāśaṅkayāha- na ceti| vyāvṛttau pramāṇābhāva-
mupapādayati-sā hīti | svasmād vyāvartayitumiti vyāvṛttatayā grahītumi-
tyarthaḥ| na caitadāśaṅkanīyam, yadi nāma parasparavyāvṛttau pramāṇābhāvaḥ, tathāpyadvaitasya
kimāyātam | na hi pramāṇābhāvāt pramāṇavyāvṛttimātreṇa prameyavyāvṛttiriti sarvathā
agṛhyamāṇāyā vyāvṛtterāśaṅkayitumapyaśakyatvāt | agrahaṇe ca sāmaggrabhāvāt| saṃbhāva-
nāyāścānupapatteriti bhāvaḥ | na caikaṃ yuktisahastreṇāpyetad buddhimadhirohati bhedasya
viṣadapratipattikatvāt ata āha- anādīti | vividheti nānārūpam| vicitreti
tadavāntarajātibahutvopetam | prapañco vistāraḥ |
nanu yathoktasamuccayavirodhitayā'dvaitaṃ nirākartavyamiti tadupanyāso'stu | sarvaṃ
dvedhetyādi tu kimarthamupanyasyate? na hi dvaitādāvapi samuccayavirodhaḥ| na, abhi-
prāyāparijñānāt | aikāntike hi dvaite dvayoḥ sadātanatve kāryopayogikādācitkatā-
sāmaggrasaṃbhava eva| anyataranivṛttāvapyekadeśasthitau punarapi sa eva doṣaḥ| tajjātī-

[p. 528]
yānyasaṃbhave cānaikāntikaṃ dvaividhyamiti virodha eva| evaṃ traividhyādāvapi draṣṭavyam|
ekavākyatā mithaḥ sahakāritetyarthaḥ| kārakavaicitryaṃ veti kartṛkaraṇādibhāvaḥ ||
sa....m|| anavayavena sarvaṃ cedekamiti na tu jātyabhedamātreṇetyarthaḥ | nanu
kimatra sādhanāntarānusaraṇaṃ svataḥ siddhatvādata āha- na ca kiñciditi| nanu para-
spareṇa saṃvedane avyavasthā syādata āha- yathā ceti | tathāpi bhedo'śakyagraha ityata
āha-na ceti| atra bhedajñānaṃ svarūpeṇaiva vā nirākriyate kāraṇanirākaraṇamukhena
vā? prathamamāśrityāha- tathā hīti | yadyapyanirvacanīyakhyātipakṣe'pi bhedajñānasya
yathoktakāraṇānupapattistulyaiva| na hyanirvacanīyakhyātiriti parasparāśrayādayo doṣā
bibhyati| tathā ca tatra yathā kāraṇasamarthanaṃ tathehāpi bhaviṣyatīti na kiñcidanupapannam,
tathāpyanirvacanīyakhyātirityapi manorathamātramityāśayavānāha - na ceti| evaṃ mu-
bhayavādisiddhe kārye tadvalenaiva kāraṇaṃ vyavasthāpyamityāha - tasmāditi| tad
vyutpādayati - tathā hīti| yathā cābhāvaḥ pratyakṣagocaraḥ, tathopapāditam| nanu bhavatu
ghaṭapaṭādīnāṃ bhedaḥ pratyakṣagocaraḥ, tathā jñānajñeyabhedo vā, tathā jñātṛjñeyabhedo vā| yastu
mitho jñātṝṇāṃ bhedaḥ sa kathamadhyakṣagocaraḥ syāt? teṣāṃ vyatisaṃvedanāsaṃbhavādata
āha-pramātāraśceti| vyavasthālakṣaṇaviruddhadharmādhyāsena teṣāmapi bhedo'numeya
ityarthaḥ| nanvekasminnapi bandhamokṣodayastvayāpyabhyupagantavyatayā na viruddhā ityata
āha-ekasminnaparyāyeṇeti | tathāpi vāstavo viruddhadharmādhyāsa ekatraikadā
virudhyate, na tu kālpanika ityata āha- kalpanayeti| kālpaniko bhedo na
virodhamāvahatītyanujānīmahe| kālpanikatvameva tvanupapannamiti bhāvaḥ ||41||
na kāraṇāvayavabhāvāt ||4|1|42||

[ phalaparīkṣāprakaraṇam ]
sadyaḥ kālāntare ca phalaniṣpatteḥ saṃśayaḥ ||4|1|44||
yāvadatra vicāritaṃ tāvadaprakṛtamapi prakṛtaṃ pretyabhāvamupakarotīti upodghātena
tat saṃgatam| phalaṃ ca lakṣaṇakramānusāreṇānantaraprakaraṇārtha ityarthaḥ| atra ca yadyapi
phalaṃ sāmānyamādāya tatkālāntarabhāvi vā syāt, sadyobhāvi veti sandeho nopapadyate|
lokapaktyādīnāṃ phalatve'nantarabhāvitvasya dharmigrāhakapramāṇanirṇītatvāt| evaṃ
svargasyāpi phalatve kālāntarabhāvitvasya ubhayavādisiddhatvāt| evaṃ sadyobhāvi-
lokapaktyādyasya phalaṃ kālāntarabhāvi svargādi vetyeṣo'pi saṃśayo'nupapanno dharmi-
bhedāt| tathāpi kriyaiva yāgādikā saṃśayaviṣayaḥ| kriyātvaṃ ca saśaṃyakāraṇam|
cetanapravṛttirhi kācit sadyaḥ phalā bhavati, yathā pacanadohanādiḥ| kācittu
kālāntarabhāviphalā bhavati karṣaṇasecanādiḥ | ataḥ prakṛte sādhakabādhakapramāṇābhāve
sati sandehaḥ| na ca paktyādyanantaropalambha eva sadyaḥ phalatvasādhaka itarasya bādhaka
iti sāṃpratam, svargaphalatvaśrutyā tasya pratihatatvāt| tarhi saiva kālāntara bhāviphalatve
kriyāyāḥ sādhikā sadyaḥ phalatvabādhikāstviti cet-na, svargasya kartṛviśeṣaṇa-
tayopāttasyāpradhānatayā āpātataḥ sādhyatvānavadhāraṇāt| tadidamuktam-etaduktaṃ

[p. 530]
bhavatīti| pūrvapakṣamāha - svargādiphalatva iti| nanu svargādiphalatve kalpanāgauravamātraṃ
sadyoduḥkhaphalatve tu cetanapravṛttyanupapattirevāta āha- tatrāpīti | nanu sāmānyato
vedaprāmāṇyasiddhau laukikapaktyādi phalamasya kalpanīyam| sarvathā niṣphalatve tu
tadanupapattireva| na ca lokapaktyādau svargapadaṃ prasiddhaprayogam| tathā ca śrutātikrama
ityata āha-tacca sukheti | siddhāntamāha - evamiti | lokapaktyādāveva kadācit
svargapadamañjasā pravartata iti pūrvapakṣiṇo'bhiprāyastatrāha - svargaśabdasyeti | nanu
candanādāvapi tatprayogadarśanāt akṣādiśabdavat svargaśabdasyāpyanekārthatā syādata
āha-na ceti| kimato yadyevamata āha- tathā ceti| mukhyārthāsaṃbhavo hyapacārabījam,
anyathātiprasaṅgāditi bhāvaḥ| nanūktam - mukhye'rthe kalpanāgauravamata āha-na
ceti| anuṣṭhānaphalamiti sattāmātrāvasthitānuṣṭhānaphalamityarthaḥ| na ceha jñāpanaṃ
śrūyate| na cāśrūyamāṇakalpanāyāṃ pramāṇamastītyabhiprāyeṇāha - adāmbhikānāmiti |
siddhe'rthe sūtraṃ yojayati-tasmāt suṣṭhaktamiti | ||44||
na sadyaḥ kālāntaropabhogyatvāt ||4|1|45||

[ duḥkhaparīkṣāprakaraṇam ]
vividhabādhanāyogād duḥkhameva janmotpattiḥ ||4|1|55||
kramaprāptaṃ duḥkhaparīkṣāprakaraṇamārabhyate| kramaprāptiśca bhāṣye pradarśitā - uktaṃ
ceti bhāṣyalakṣitamityarthaḥ | nanu kimiha parīkṣaṇīyam ? na hi duḥkhasvarūpe kaścid
vipratipadyate | nāpi taddheyamaheyaṃ veti kecit sandihate| nityamanityaṃ veti tu parīkṣā
yadyapi prakṛtopayoginī, tannityatve hi śāstrānarthakyaprasaṅgaḥ | asti ca saṃśayakāraṇaṃ
vādivipratipattiḥ | sāṃkhyā hi sarvanityatvavādino duḥkhamapi nityamāhuḥ | tathāpi
tanmatanirāsādanantaraṃ ca satkāryavādaniṣedhādiyamapi niravaśeṣitā| kāraṇaṃ ca
duḥkhasya dṛṣṭarūpamahiviṣakaṇṭakādi na vipratipattiviṣayaḥ| adṛṣṭarūpā ca pravṛttiḥ
parīkṣitaiva| tadavāntarakāryarūpaṃ ca phalaṃ janmādyapi parīkṣitameva| kāraṇanivṛttau ca
kāryaṃ nivartata iti dvitīyasūtre parīkṣitam| tatkimaparamavaśiṣyate yadarthamayamā-
rambha ityata āha-tatreti |
bādhanālakṣaṇaṃ duḥkham
iti lakṣitam| tatra bādhanāśabdena bādhanābuddhirucyate| tayā lakṣyamāṇaṃ viṣayī-
kriyamāṇamiti lakṣaṇārthaḥ| anena ca lakṣaṇena duḥkhāyatanāni duḥkhasādhanāni sukhaṃ

[p. 535]
ca vyāpyate| tatra ca mukhyāṃ bādhanabuddhimupādāya pūrvapakṣāvakāśaḥ| tadidamuktam-tat
kathaṃ duḥkhamanubhavavirodhāditi ? satpratipakṣaṃ cedaṃ lakṣaṇam, duḥkhānuṣaṅgād
duḥkhatve sukhānuṣaṅgāt sukhatvasyāpi prasakteḥ | tadidamāha - śarīrendriyeti |
tasmānmukhyasyaiva duḥkhasya lakṣaṇaṃ kartumucitam, lakṣaṇavyavasthopapatteḥ| loka-
vyavahārabādhitaṃ caitallakṣaṇamityāha - seyamatibhīruteti| na ca sukhasyāpi duḥkha-
tvopacāre kiñcit prayojanamasti| tathā sati duḥkhahānamasya sidhyatīti prayojanam|
taccānyathāpi sidhyatītyāha - tasmānmāṃsārthīveti | so'yaṃ pūrvapakṣo na sūtrabhāṣya-
vārttikeṣvastītyata āha-ityabhiprāyavāniti | tatra mukhyaṃ lakṣaṇārthamāśritya
yaduktaṃ tatrāha - na vai sarveti | prayojanābhāve uttaramāha - duḥkhahānārtha iti| satprati-
pakṣatvaṃ pariharati-duḥkhaṃ jihāsata iti| etena lokavyavahārabādho'pi parihṛto
boddhavyaḥ| tadidaṃ sphuṭayati - taduktaṃ bhavatīti | tadevamarthagatiṃ pariśodhya
sūtramavatārayati-atra ceti ||55||
na sukhasyāpyantarālaniṣpatteḥ ||4|1|56||
atra niruktamanyatamamutthāpya sūtreṇa nirācaṣṭe - kasmāt punariti ||56||
bādhanānivṛttervedayataḥ paryeṣaṇadoṣādapratiṣedhaḥ ||4|1|57||

[ apavargaparīkṣāprakaraṇam ]
ṛṇakleśapravṛttyanubandhādapavargābhāvaḥ ||4|1|59||
kramaprāptamidānīṃ mokṣalakṣaṇaparīkṣāprakaraṇamārabhyate,
tadatyantavimokṣo'pavargaḥ.
iti ca lakṣaṇam| lakṣaṇavākyatvādeva cātra saṃśayaḥ, upapannārthamanupapannārthaṃ veti|
āśrayāsiddhayā pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ| ātyantikī duḥkhanivṛttihyapavargo vivakṣitaḥ | tathā
cāyamarthaḥ, apavarga itarebhyo bhidyate apavarga iti vyavahartavyo vā, ātyantikaduḥ-
khocchedarūpatvāt | yastu taditarebhyo na bhidyate, na vā tathā vyavahriyate nāsāvevaṃrūpo
yathā saṃsāraḥ| evaṃrūpaścārtho na pratyakṣasiddhaḥ| tathā ca santānānumānāgamāvatra pramāṇe
syātām| taccobhayamapyanuṣṭhānakālātipātena pratihataṃ sannātmānamāsādayati| tathā
hi nāyaṃ saṃsārī tattvajñānānuṣṭhānādikrameṇa kadācidapavṛjyate, ṛṇakleśapravṛttyanubaddhatvāt
gatakālasaṃsārivat| tadetat sarvamucyata ityāha-duḥkhoddeśeti |
ṛṇa.......vaḥ || nanu kleśānubandhādapavargābhāva ityuktam| sarvathā kleśānubandhābhāve
hi saṃsāra eva na syāt| tathā ca kuto'pavarga ityata āha- nānucchinneti | na ca
nidānamucchettuṃ śakyam, tadvirodhitattvajñānotpādanāvasarābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ | uttaratrāpi śaṅkā
bhāvaśca tādṛśāveva | ||59 ||
pradhānaśabdānupapatterguṇaśabdenārthavādo nindāpraśaṃsopapatteḥ
||4|1|60||
nanu ṛṇaśabdasya mukhyārthatvavad gauṇārthatve'pi na kaścit siddhāntopakāra
ityata āha-jāyamāna itīti |

[p. 537]
pra....  || nanu kāmasaṃyogaśrutyā tadā bālakasyānadhikāro gamyate| yadi tasya
kāmavirodhaḥ syāt na caivam| so'pi hi hitāhitaprāptiparihārau kāmayata evetyata
āha-na ceti| avidvattvādineti | atrādigrahaṇena śarīrasāmarthyasaṃskārakarmasādhana-
dravyopādānavidhayo gṛhyante| iyatā prabandhena siddhaṃ siddhāntaviṣayamāha- tadaneneti|
vakṣyamāṇasiddhāntaviṣayamāha - saṃpratīti | nanu jāyamāna ityādi bālakasya yajñādi
vidhāsyatīti śaṅkā arthinaḥ śaktasya cādhikārādityādinā pūrvameva nirākṛtā|
tad utthānamevāsya nāstītyata āha- asti ceti| nanu tathāpi sādhanānuṣṭhānayogyatā
nāstītyuktamata āha-teneti | nanu kiṃ tayetyata āha-phalena ceti| yathokte
siddhānto na 'niviśata ityata āha- ayamabhiprāya iti| abhinirvacanīya iti
abhivyāpanīya ityarthaḥ| tadidamuktam- tadupāyo viṣaya iti| kathamenaṃ prayatnena
vyāpnuyāditi ca| nanūktaṃ sarvajñasya sarvārthopadeśārthaṃ pravṛttasya bhagavata īśvarasya
vidhānābhāvāt| aśruto'pi niṣedho'numāsyata ityata āha-na ceti | nanu
nityaprakāśamiti vyākhyānaṃ svacaritaviruddhamata āha- tadaneneti | phalagocarā
svarasavāhinī icchā kāmaḥ| tenopādhinā sādhanagocarā anenedaṃ kuryāmityākārā ca
saṃkalpa ityapaunaruktyam| na tu prayatnaḥ tat karma kuruta iti dhātvarthasya karotyarthatvāt|
taduktam-
yādṛśo'sya bhavet kāmaḥ tādṛśo jāyate kratuḥ |
kratuniścaya evātra tataḥ karma pravartate ||
iti bhāvaḥ ||60||

samāropaṇādātmanyapratiṣedhaḥ ||4|1|61||
pratyakṣavidhānaṃ pravrajyāyā apīti | pratyakṣavidhānamapi pravrajyāyā
ityarthaḥ ||61||
pātracayāntānupapatteśca phalābhāvaḥ ||4|1|62||
nanu
samāropaṇādātmanyapratiṣedhaḥ
ityanena sūtreṇa pravrajyāyā vidhānaṃ darśayatā anavasaradoṣo nirākṛtaḥ|
pātracayāntānupapatteśca phalābhāvaḥ
ityanenāpi sa eveti paunaruktyamityata āha- tadaneneti| pūrveṇa pravrajyāvidhāna-
śrutirdarśitā, anena tvaviśeṣeṇa maraṇaparyante karmavidhāne śrutyantaravirodha ityarthaḥ|
dārdhyārthamiti saṃskārasyeti śeṣaḥ ||62||
suṣuptasya svapnādarśane kleśābhāvavadapavargaḥ ||4|1|63||
kathaṃ tarhi suṣuptasya pralīnasya vā niṣkleśatve'pi punarāvṛttiḥ, apunarāvṛttiśca
muktasyetyata āha-etāvāṃstviti ||63||

[ doṣanimittatāprakaraṇam ]
doṣanimittanāṃ tattvajñānādahaṅkāranivṛttiḥ ||4|2|1 ||
evaṃ prathamāhnike caturdaśabhiḥ prakaraṇaiḥ kāryarūpapravṛttyādiprameyaṣaṭkaṃ parīkṣitam |
idānīṃ tasya dharmastattvajñānaṃ parīkṣaṇīyam-kiṃviṣayaṃ kiṃlakṣaṇaṃ kathamutpadyate kathaṃ
paripālyate kathaṃ vivardhate ceti tattvajñānaparīkṣaivāhnikārthaḥ | tatra yasmin viṣaye
yathotpadyate tattvajñānaṃ tatkramapratipādanārthaṃ (1) prathamaṃ doṣanimittatāprakaraṇam, (2)
dvitīyaṃ prāsaṅgikamavayaviprakaraṇam, (3) tṛtīyamaupoddhātikaṃ niravayavaprakaraṇam,
(4) caturthaṃ prāsaṅgikaṃ bāhyārthaprakaraṇam, (5) pañcamaṃ tattvajñānavivṛddhiprakaraṇam,
(6) ṣaṣṭhaṃ tattvajñānarakṣāprakaraṇamiti ṣaṭ prakaraṇāni | ṣoḍaśāpīti | nanu
vādajalpavitaṇḍānāmagre parīkṣiṣyamāṇatvāt kathametat ? caturthaśeṣaṃ pratisandhāya
vakṣyati ca pañcame - jalpavitaṇḍāparīkṣā cānantaraṃ vṛtteti | na, tattvajñānaparipālanasya
tatra pradhānatvāt| jalpavitaṇḍāparīkṣā cānantaramityetasya cādūraviprakṛṣṭārthatvāt |
na hi tatra 'kathālakṣaṇopapattyanupapatticarcāsti | tato
yatra saṃśayastatraivamuttarottaraprasaṅga
ityatidiṣṭaparīkṣāsaṃbandhādeva kathā parīkṣiteti yuktamuktam ṣoḍaśāpi parīkṣiteti |
yadyapi kiṃ nu khalu bhorityādau bhāṣye pramāṇādipraśne'pi na kaścid doṣaḥ, tathāpi
prameyatattvajñānārthatvāt teṣāṃ svātantryeṇa na kaścidupayoga iti svatantropayogiṣu
prameyeṣveva vicāro'stvityedarthaṃ pīṭhabandhaḥ tatrāpīti | uttaravādinaḥ praśno'nupapanna
1. °papattiranupapattirvāsti M

[p. 542]
eveti tena viśeṣasyābhidhāsyamānatvāt, aviśeṣasya ca tatrāvivakṣitatvāt| ata
āha-pārśvastha iti| nanu mithyājñānaṃ vā moho'stu, tattvajñānānutpattirvā| ko
viśeṣo yena tattvajñānānutpattiparīkṣāparīhāreṇa mithyājñānaṃ moha iti vyutpādyate ata
āha-na tattvajñānamiti| dvayaṃ hi tattvajñānasya viruddham, svaprāgabhāvo mithyājñānaṃ
ca| tatra svaprāgabhāvanivṛttirūpatāsya yadyapi prakṛtopayoginī svarūpasya tathābhāvāt,
tathāpi na tāvanmātraṃ mithyājñānasādhāraṇyāt| kintu mithyājñānavirodhirūpatā
tadupadarśitārthāsahiṣṇusvabhāvateti yāvaditi mātraśabdārthaḥ | bhavatviti| bhavatu
moho mithyājñānam, tathāpi siddhānte kimāyātam, paramātmādāvapi tasya saṃbhavādi-
tyarthaḥ| svasaṃbandhina iti| ātmānaṃ prati svasaṃbandhaḥ śarīramābhimānikamātmānamādāya
samarthanīyaḥ| kecidāhurityādau yathopadarśitattattvajñānaviparyayeṇa mithyājñānaviprati-
pattayo boddhavyāḥ| dharmapudgaleti| dharmanairātmyaṃ bhāvanairātmyaṃ pudgalanairātmyaṃ tīrthikā-
bhimatātmani:svabhāvatvamiti | nanu sāmānādhikaraṇyaśrutistarhi virudhyate| ata
āha-satyamiti | kathañciditi| vijānātīti vijñānam| ānandayatīti ānandaḥ
kartṛvyutpattyarthaḥ| pareṣāṃ laukikaṃ dṛṣṭāntaṃ dūṣayati-na ca prakṛṣṭa iti| nanu prakṛte-
rasaṃbhavāditi heturasiddhaḥ, viśvamūlakāraṇasyāsmābhirabhyupagamāt| viśvātmikāyāstu
sāṃkhyābhimatāyāḥ prakṛterasiddhau tadabhāvasyāpyasiddheḥ| pratiyoginirūpaṇa-
mantareṇābhāvasya nirūpayitumaśakyatvādata āha- satkāryeti| kāryasya hi nivṛtta-
syāpi kāraṇātmanāvasthānam, kāraṇātmanāvasthitasya ca punarāvirbhāva ityanena
nyāyena yatra viśvaṃ pralīyate tanmūlakāraṇaṃ prakṛtiriti ucyate| tato mūlakāraṇe
sāmānyataḥ siddhe, viśvātmakaṃ tadanyathā veti vicāre yadvāreṇa tasya tathātvaṃ
setsyati tasya satkāryavādasya pratiṣedhāt tadanātmakatvaṃ dharmigrāhakapramāṇasiddha-
miti bhāvaḥ | yasmāduktarūpaparadarśanaviparyayo na mohaḥ, tasmādityuttaravākya-

viruddho hetuḥ| na hi cintāvyatirekiṇi cittasvarūpe gṛhītikarmatā astītyata

labhyate, tathāpi viśeṣaṃ vaktuṃ tāṃ pṛthagāha - api ceti| viśeṣamāha - vartamāneti |
nanu na jñānenāhitamarthe śuklatvādivat kañcid dharmamupalabhāmahe| ato'nupalabdhibādhita
evāyamartho na śaṅkāmapyadhirohati, ityata āha- na caitaditi | nanvatītānāgata-
yorapyarthayoḥ sāmānyākāreṇa vartamānatāmicchanto mīmāṃsakā naivamupālambhamarhantītyata
āha-na khalviti | bhavatu teṣāmapi sāmānyākāreṇa vartamānatā| na tu tenākāreṇa
prākaṭyalakṣaṇaṃ dharmaṃ prati tasya dharmitā yujyate, jñānasyātītānāgatagocaratvānupa-
patteḥ| na hyanyādhārayā jñātatayānyaviṣayatvaṃ jñānasya| tādātmyābhyupagame'pi

[p. 552]
rūpasaṃvedanasyāpi rasaviṣayatāpatterityarthaḥ| na cāsatyapi niyāmakāntare svabhāvaniyato
viṣayaviṣayibhāvaḥ sādhāraṇyaprasaṅgādityabhiprāyavānupasaṃharati- tasmāditi|
ajaḍenetyasya vivaraṇaṃ prakāśātmaneti| manaskārendriyādibhiriti manaskāra-
viṣayavadindriyasyāpi cakṣurāderutpādakatvaṃ viṣayapratiniyamaśca tulyāviti darśayitumi-
hendriyopādānam| ādiśabdena cālokādigrahaṇam| tadetat pūrvatrāpi draṣṭavyam|
nanu vikalpya śaṅkarācāryeṇa dūṣitametat| nīlādīnāmapratipattāvāśrayāsiddhatayā
pratipattau dharmigrāhakapramāṇabādhitayetyata āha-nīlādayo hīti | tathāpi sa
doṣastadavastha eva vyatirekāpratipattau tatra pratiṣedhānupapatteḥ | tatpratītau vā tathaiva
bādhāvakāśādetadāha - parairabhyupagata iti| evaṃ bhavatetyādi niyogavivaraṇam|
kasmānnaivamityādi paryanuyogavivaraṇam| nirākṛtamapyarthaṃ viśeṣavivecanāya
punarāśaṅkate -akiñcitkarasyeti| nanu karmakriyayorekatvamāsthitaṃ pareṇa, yenaivamupā-
labhyetetyata āha-idamatrākūtamiti | aindriyakaśca bāhyendriyajanyaśca ityarthaḥ|
jñānaprakāśādhīnaprakāśāśca viṣayaprakāśā ityupanītaṃ hetuṃ vikalpagarbha daṣayati-na
ca pramāṇeti | ādhyātmika iti vāsanārūpa ityarthaḥ ||33||
smṛtisaṅkalpavacca svapnaviṣayābhimānaḥ ||4|2|34||
nanu nākāraśabdena parasya sādṛśyaṃ vivakṣitam kintu svarūpam| ato
yathoktavyāghātānavakāśa ityata āha- ayamabhisandhiriti | astu tarhi viśeṣaṇameva
kevalaṃ hetuḥ| nanu madīyāhaṅkārānāspadatvaṃ prakṛtasādhyavyabhicāri| tato'samarthaviśeṣaṇa-
tvam ata āha-āropeti| ayamarthaḥ | yadyapi yathokto heturna prakṛtasādhyavyabhicārī,
tathāpyātmaivānātmatayā sphuratīti pakṣe cintāvyatireke'pyahaṅkārānāspadatvamupapadyamānaṃ
sandigdhavipakṣavyāvṛttitāṃ nātikrāmatīti tadarthamidantāspadasya satyatvamupādeyam|

vivekamupakurvantīti bhedamātramāsāmuktamiti| asyāyamarthaḥ| nāhatya tattvajñānaṃ

lakṣaṇamātramuktam| mātraśabdena parīkṣāṃ vyavacchinatti| tamimaṃ nyāyasiddhamarthamadhyāyā-
rthamupasaṃharannāha -
jātīnāṃ saprapañcānām

prakṛtasādhye'pyaprayojakatvaprasaṅgāt| tatpratipattijanakatvameva hi tasya tatra prayoja-
katvamityabhimānino jātivādinaḥ| atastadeva nibandhanaṃ tatprayogasādhyatvāpādana
iti ||4||
kiñcitsādharmyādupasaṃhārasiddhevaidharmyādapratiṣedhaḥ ||5|1|5||
sādhyātideśācca dṛṣṭāntopapatteḥ ||5|1|6||
upapadyate upapanno bhavatīti ṭīkā jñāto bhavatītyarthaḥ| tanaitaduktaṃ bhavati | na
sattāmātravyavasthiteḥ vivakṣitatayopasaṃhriyate| api tu vyāptatayā jñāta iti tātparyam|
atinirbandheneti| ayaṃ nirbandhārthaḥ | vibhāgajatvāvibhāgajatvavikalpavannityā-
nityavikalpo'pi vyavatiṣṭhatāmityevaṃ vadataḥ ko'bhiprāyaḥ? kiṃ vibhāgajatvādau
dharmāntare kṛtakatvaṃ ced vyabhicaritamato'nityatvamapi vyabhicaret āhosvid
vibhāgajatvāvibhāgajatvaprayukte nityatvānityatve, tataḥ kṛtakatvamaprayojakamiti |
tatra prathame sautramuttaram| dvitīye tvidaṃ vārttikamupatiṣṭhata iti||5-6||
|| iti sādhyadṛṣṭāntadharmavikalpaprabhavotkarṣasamādijātiṣaṭkaprakaraṇam ||
[ prāptyaprāptiyuganaddhavāhivikalpopakramajāti-
dvayaprakaraṇam ]
prāpya sādhyamaprāpya vā hetoḥ prāptyāviśiṣṭatvādaprāptyāsādha-
katvācca prāptyaprāptisamau ||5|1|7||
lakṣyapadamityatra lakṣyayoḥ padamiti vigrahaḥ| sādhyamiha phalaṃ karma vā, tatra
1. °bhicaratu JA

[p. 571]
phalābhiprāyeṇāha-asat sādhyate na tu sat prāptaṃ ca saditi | tato'sattvaprāptī
parasparaviruddhe ityarthaḥ| karmābhiprāyeṇāha- api cetyādi | sādhanapratiṣedhasyai-
katve'pīti yuganaddhavāhitāmātramekatvamiha draṣṭavyam| vastugatyā tvaviśeṣāti-
prasaṅgayo pratiṣedhayorbhinnatvāt vikalpabhedād bhedavivakṣetyādivikalpayorvirodhā-
dekenottareṇa nirākartumaśakyatvena bhedavivakṣāparasparāsaṅkīrṇottaraṃ vivakṣetyarthaḥ| tathā
ca vikalpayoryuganaddhavāhitve'pi guṇapradhānabhāvena jātyuttarayoḥ pravṛttāvekodbhāva-
naniyamaḥ saṅgacchata ityabhiprāyaḥ ||7||
ghaṭādiniṣpattidarśanāt pīḍane cābhicārādapratiṣedhaḥ ||5|1|8||
ghaṭastu phalaṃ na sādhya iti na sādhanakarmetyarthaḥ| na ca phalena sādhanasya
prāptiḥ, phalasiddhāvaṅgamitaretarāśrayaprasaṅgāt | niyamastu svabhāvaniyamādeveti bhāva-
śeṣaḥ| atrāpi saṃyuktasaṃyogādiḥ sambandha upeya iti lābhasaukaryeṇoktam, uddeśa-
syaiva niyāmakatvāt| niyamārthaṃ ca sambandhānusaraṇāt| saṃyuktasaṃyogādestvaniyāma-
katvāt| prāptau cāviśeṣeṇa sādhakatvavyāghātaḥ prāptisamaḥ| aprāptāvatiprasaṅgena
sādhakatvakhaṇḍanamaprāptisamaḥ| ubhāvapyetau pratikūlatarkābhāsau | kṛtijñaptisādhāraṇa-
tvamanayorbhāṣyavārttikayoreva sphuṭataram| yaduktam, na hyaprāptaḥ pradīpaḥ prakāśaya-
tīti bhāṣyam, vārttikañca - na hyagniraprāpto dahatīti | sā ceyaṃ
kāryakāraṇāpavādadvāreṇa pravartamāneti ca vārttikam ||8||
|| iti prāpyaprāptiyuganaddhavāhivikalpopakramajātidvayaprakaraṇam ||

2. ° śrayatvapra°

[p. 580]
vāstavābhimānikavyavahartavyānekatvena sādhanāṃśe saṃśayya tannirṇayāya svo-
tprekṣitapramāṇadūṣaṇena pratyavasthānaṃ kāryasamaḥ| maule 'cāyamasiddhadeśanābhāsaḥ| ata
eva bhāṣyakāro maulaṃ hetuṃ vivṛṇvannāha - tatkhalu abhūtvā bhavatīti| etasya
sādhyaṃ vivṛṇoti anityamiti ca [bhūtvā] na bhavatīti| tadidamabhūtvā bhāvitvaṃ
sandigdhaṃ satprayatnānantaramupalabdheḥ sādhanīyamityabhipretya jātivādyāha, prayatnānantara-
mātmalābhaśca dṛṣṭo ghaṭādīnāmupalabdhānāmiti śeṣaḥ| vyavadhānāpohāccābhivyakti-
vryavahitatānām| prakṛte yojayati - tat kimityādi| vārttikakāro'pi maulasya
kāryatvahetorasiddhimāśaṅkayāha, kāryaḥ śabdaḥ prayatnānantaramupalabdheriti hetumanaikāntikaṃ
deśayati| prayatnānantaramupalabhyamānaṃ kāryamakāryaṃ ca dṛṣṭamiti| eṣa ca heturanena
svayamevotprekṣito na vādyabhimata iti| pūrvameva prayatnānantaropalabdherhetu-
tvādhyāropeṇeti vadatā suvyañjitam| tadetadasya jātyuttarasyāyameva viṣaya iti bhramaṃ
tāvannivārayati ṭīkākṛt- udāharaṇapūrvakamiti | mūlamūlisaṅkarabhramaṃ nivāra-
yati-kāryatvādanityaḥ śabda iti| asiddhatvāt sādhyāviśiṣṭatvāt| tasmāt
prayatnānantaropalambha āstheyaḥ| kāryatvasiddhaya iti śeṣaḥ| suvyaktamanyat| bauddhānāṃ
tu kāryasamavyutpādanaṃ svacaritaviruddhamityāha | tadaneneti| etāvatāpi tallakṣaṇaṃ na
dūṣitamata āha-na ceyamiti | yadi daṇḍacakrādivyāpāra  pūrvakatvaṃ śabde
prāsādādivaccharīravyāpārapūrvakatvaṃ ca kṣityādāvupanayati tadotkarṣasamatvamiti |
atha tadapāyāt kṣityādau  kāryatvamevāpanayati tadāpakarṣasamatvam| anyatvamātreṇa
ca pratyavasthāne varṇyavarṇyasamayoranyataratrāntarbhavatīti draṣṭavyam||37-38||
|| iti kāryasamaprakaraṇam ||

[ ṣaṭpakṣīrūpakathābhāsaprakaraṇam ]
pratiṣedhe'pi samāno doṣaḥ ||5|1|39||
sarvatraivam ||5|1|40||
pratiṣedhavipratiṣedhe pratiṣedhadoṣavad doṣaḥ ||5|1|41||
pratiṣedhaṃ sadoṣamabhyupetya pratiṣedhavipratiṣedhe samāno doṣaprasaṅgo
matānujñā ||5|1|42||
svapakṣalakṣaṇāpekṣopapattyupasaṃhāre hetunirdeśe parapakṣadoṣābhyupa-
gamāt samāno doṣaḥ ||5|1|43||1
caturthaṣaṣṭhayorarthāviśeṣāt punaruktadoṣaprasaṅgaḥ samānaḥ | tṛtīyapañcamayoḥ punarukta-
doṣaprasaṅga ityete sūtre iti keṣāñcid bhramaḥ | taṃ nivārayati | bhāṣyavyākhyayā
vārttikamapi vyākhyātamiti praviśiṣṭaṃ tu pariśiṣṭe |
nanu jātīnāṃ vibhāgoddeśo'nupapannaḥ | caturviṃśateranyāsāmapi sambhavāt| tad
yathā vikalpasamāviparyayeṇāvikalpasamā | tathā hi nedaṃ nirātmakaṃ jīvaccharīraṃ
prāṇādimattvāt| yatpunarnirātmakaṃ na tat prāṇādimat yathā ghaṭa iti prayukte
jātivādyāha- prāṇādimattve tadvirahalakṣaṇavaidharmyavyavasthitāvapi ghaṭajīvaccharīra-
yoryathā na sadasattvavikalpaḥ, tathā sātmakatve nairātmyavikalpo na bhaviṣyati|
1. pariśuddhau ṣaṭpakṣīsūtrāṇi na vyākhyātāni |

3. sātmakatvanairātmyakavikalpo'pi JA

seyamavikalpasamā| evaṃ sādhyasamāviparyayeṇāsādhyasamā, tadyathā sādhyadharmi-
dṛṣṭāntadharmiṇostulyatve dṛṣṭāntavat sādhyasyāpi pañcāvayavaprayogāt sādhyatvaprasaṅga
iti|
tathā prasaṅgasamāviparyayeṇāprasaṅgasamā| tadyathā anavasthābhayena pramāṇaṃ tāvat
pramāṇamantareṇaiva siddhyatītyabhyupagantavyam| tathā ca pramāṇavat prameyasyāpi
svataḥ siddhiprasaṅgaḥ |
evaṃ pratyakṣasamopamānasamāśabdasamāḥ pratyakṣābhāsādi bādhapratirodhayorudbhāvane
draṣṭavyāḥ| evamavyāptisamā| tadyathā dhūmānalayordṛśyayorvā vyāptiradṛśyayorvā dṛśyā-
dṛśyasādhāraṇayorveti vikalpāḥ| iha 'dṛśyavyāptau kārīṣo na siddhayet| adṛśyavyāptau
ca tārṇādi na siddhayet| sādhāraṇavyāptau caitrakajāṭharāvapi siddhayetāmityādi| evaṃ
prativandisamā tad yathā kāryatvād buddhimattvasiddhau śarīrāderapi siddhiprasaṅgaḥ|
buddhinityatve tadanupayogavad vā prayatnanityatve buddherapyasiddhiprasaṅgaḥ| evaṃ śaṅkā-
piśācīsamā| sā tu prasiddhaiva| evaṃ viparītaprasaktisamā tadyathā aghaṭaṃ bhūtalamiti
sādhye nāprasaktaṃ pratiṣidhyate kintu prasaktam| prasaktiśca jñānam| tathā ca niṣedhakaṃ
pramāṇamanupalabdhilakṣaṇaṃ nāstīti| evamanupakārisamā| tadyathā kāryakāraṇabhāvā-
dāvupakāraniyamo vā syādanyathā veti vikalpe upakāre'pyupakārāntarāpekṣāyā
anavasthānāt| dvitīye tu sarvaṃ sarvasya kāraṇaṃ kāryaṃ ca syāditi| evaṃ siddhasādhanasamā
tadyathā buddhimatpūrvakamiti sādhye mīmāṃsakānāṃ karmabhiḥ sarvajīvānāṃ tatsiddheḥ
siddhasādhanāpādanamiti| evaṃ saṃśayasamāviparyayeṇāsaṃśayasamā| tad yathā sandigdhe
nyāyena pravartitavyam| tatra  svātmani saṃśayastādrūpyeṇa vartate na vā| prathame na pakṣaḥ
sandigdhaḥ saṃśayasyaiva sandigdhatvāt| dvitīye tu saṃśayo na syādeva| svātmani

tvamāpādayet, tadā anityasameti| asādhyasamā tu nāstyeva bījābhāvenāsambhava-

4 ṣaphalaprasaṅga

[p. 588]
dvāviṃśatyāmapi nigrahasthāneṣu 'vipratipattirapratipattiśca saṃpnavata iti yadāpi 'hyapratibhā
tadāpi na vādī svapiti kintu kiñcitpratipadyata eva| sā ca tasya pratipattiḥ|
prakṛtārthaviruddhatayā vipratipattirucyate| evamapratipattirapi yathāvat prakṛtārtha-
gocarapratipattyabhāvarūpā sarvāṇyeva nigrahasthānānyanudhāvati| bhrāmyannapi hi tattvaṃ na
pratipadyata eva|  tasmādajñānatvadurjñānatvarūpasāmānyadvaividhyavivakṣayā lakṣaṇadvayena
mahāsāmānyameva lakṣitamityarthaḥ | vacanadvāreṇeti vacanādyavakturdoṣā ityarthaḥ| tenā-
pratibhādayo'pi saṃgṛhyante| ato na cāyaṃ niyama ityādinā pūrvoktenāvirodhaḥ|
pratijñāhānirityādyasamāsaḥ svaprayojane nigrahasthānānāṃ parasparānapekṣatvasūcanāyeti
cet jātiṣvapyetat samānamityasamāsaprasaktiriti śrīvatsaḥ| tatrottaram|
samaśabdābhyāvṛttau sūtragauravāt| anāvṛttau tu saṃjñānupapatteśca samāsaḥ||1||
pratidṛṣṭāntadharmābhyanujñā svadṛṣṭānte pratijñāhāniḥ ||5|2|2||

hyātmaikaguṇatvena sukhādīnāmapi nityatvaṃ prasajyeta| kṣaṇikaṃ śabdādi sattvādityā-
dyanukūlatarkaśūnyaṃ sthairye'pyavirodhāditi |
atrocyate| aprayojakastāvadasiddhabheda eva| vyāptatayā hi pakṣe pratītiḥ
siddhirityucyate| tadabhāvo'siddhiḥ | sā ca vyāptipakṣasvarūpāṇāmanyatamāpratītyāpi
bhavantī anyathāsiddhirāśrayāsiddhiḥ svarūpāsiddhirityākhyāyate| anyathāsiddho'-
prayojakaśceti paryāyau| evamasamarthaviśeṣaṇādayo'pasiddhabhedāḥ | tadeva hi viśeṣaṇaṃ
viśeṣyamubhayaṃ vā liṅgatvena prayoktavyam, yajjñātaṃ sadanumitiṃ bhāvayet tadeva ca tathā
yad vyabhicāraṃ viruṇaddhi| asamarthaṃ ca na vyabhicāraviruddhatayā jñātamato'siddha-
bhedāḥ| siddhabhedaḥ siddhasādhanamapyāśrayāsiddhibhedaḥ | sandigdhasādhyadharmāhetorāśraya
iṣyate| na ca nirṇīte saṃśayo'stīti| na caivaṃ sati bādhitaviṣayasyāpyatraivāntarbhāvaḥ
śaṅkitavyo viparītasiddhervyāptivighātarūpatayā svarūpeṇaiva dūṣaṇatvāt | sādhyasiddhestu
pratibandhāviruddhatayā svarūpeṇādūṣaṇatvāt| apratītavyāptikasyāpyasiddhe'ntarbhāvaḥ
siddhirahitatvāt | vyāptatayā pakṣe 'pratīte'siddheruktatvāt| anenaiva nyāyenānupasaṃ-
hāryo'pyasiddhaḥ | sarvapakṣīkaraṇe prameyatvāderātmādau bhāge dharmigrāhakapramāṇabādhaḥ,
śabdādau siddhasādhanaṃ ca| evaṃ viparītatarkapratihatānukūlatarkarahitayorapyasiddhatva-
mevāvaseyam| sattarkabhūyodarśanagamyāyā vyāpterasati tarke'grahāditi |
syādetat| tathāpi sādhanavikalasādhyavikalobhayavikalāḥ sādhyavyāvṛttasādhana-
vyāvṛttobhayavyāvṛttāḥ āśrayāsiddhāśca dṛṣṭāntābhāsāḥ| anupadarśitānvayaviparītopa-
darśitānvayānupadarśitavyatire kaviparītopadarśitavyatirekāścodāharaṇābhāsāḥ
aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇāprasiddhaviśeṣyāprasiddhobhayāśca pakṣābhāsāḥ kvāntarbhāvanīyāḥ ? na
kvāpītyeke | ādhikye tarhi vibhāgabhaṅgaprasaṅga iti cet, na | anuktasamuccayārthena
cakāreṇa samuccitatvāt| kaṇṭhata eva kimiti noktā iti cet na, sūtrakṛtervaicitryāt |
1. pratītiḥ siddherityuktatvāt JA

[p. 601]
vaiśeṣike guṇavibhāgavaditi| tadasat, 'evamapi hetvābhāsa evāntarbhāvāt| tathā hi
sādhanavikale dṛṣṭānte yathāprayogaṃ heturasādhāraṇānaikāntikaḥ syāt | sādhyavikale
viruddhaḥ| ubhayavikale ca pratibandhaḥ| pratipattau vyāpyatvāsiddhaḥ| sādhyavyāvṛttau ca
sādhāraṇaḥ| sādhanavyāvṛttau viruddhaḥ| ubhayavyāvṛttau vyatirekaḥ | vyāptyasiddhau vyā-
pyatvāsiddhaḥ | āśrayāsiddhe'pi tathā| anupadarśitānvayasyāpi vastutaḥ siddhasyāpi
pratibandhasyānupadarśanāddheturvyāpyatvāsiddhaḥ | tathāvidhānāmudāharaṇābhāsānāṃ hetvā-
dyanākāṅkṣitatvenānanvayādapārthakatvaṃ ca| aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇādau tu pakṣābhāse'pakṣa-
dharmatāhetoḥ "pratipāditaiveti |
syādetat| ātmāśrayaparasparāśrayacakrakavṛttyanavasthāḥ kvāntarbhavanti? eṣāṃ
pratikūlatarkarūpatayā tatpratihatasya sādhanasya vyāpyatvāsiddhatvaṃ tāvat sarvasādhāraṇam|
viśeṣatastvātmāśraye parasparāśraye cakrakavṛttau svarūpāsiddhiranavasthāyāṃ
cāśrayāsiddhirbādhavyabhicārāviti | yadyevametāni yathāyathamukteṣvevāntarbhavanti
kimanuktaṃ yatsamuccayārthatayā ṭīkākṛtā cakāro vyākhyāta iti cenna, eteṣāmeva
pṛthagvyavahartavyatāmātramanuktam| tatsamuccayārthaścakāra iti sarvaṃ nistaraṅgamiti ||24||

1. eṣāmapi hetvābhāsāntarbhāvāt JA 2. A sādhyasādhanobhayavyāvṛttyāśrayāsiddhānāṃ
vaidharmyadṛṣṭāntābhāsānāṃ tu yathākramamudāharaṇāni yathā - śabdo nityaḥ amūrtatvāt, paramāṇuvat, karmavat,
nabhovat, vājiviṣāṇavat| prayojanavikalpe hi dṛṣṭānte asādhāraṇyaṃ hetoḥ tatrānvaye sādhanaṃ prayojanaṃ
vyatirekasādhyavyāvṛttiḥ prayojakāḥ| B₁ sādhanasādhyobhayavikalānāṃ dṛṣṭāntābhāsānāṃ
